# Agents · D · part 7

1,062 entries, deal-reviewer to debugger. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **deal-reviewer**: Deal reviewer for an organization on GTM OS. Use for "review this deal", "which deals are at risk", "deal risks", a weekly deal review, or before a forecast call. Reads the deal, its relationship history, calls, replies and score.committee_coverage, flags risks with the record behind each, creates next-step tasks and proposes stage moves and CRM write-backs for approval. ([ay-automate/gtmos-skills](https://github.com/ay-automate/gtmos-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/deal-reviewer.md))
- **deal-rule-engine**: Deal Desk pricing and rule engine specialist. Use proactively when changing TCV calculation, payment fees, debit regulation, profitability margins, deal validation, status rules, or dynamic SpEL rule definitions. Use for rule debugging, edge-case test design, and assessing financial-calculation blast radius before edits. ([drgaciw/deal-desk-service](https://github.com/drgaciw/deal-desk-service/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/deal-rule-engine.md))
- **deal-scorer**: Fast deal qualification scorer — BANT assessment with weighted composite score and GO/WAIT/KILL verdict. ([rp4ri/claude-local-marketplace](https://github.com/rp4ri/claude-local-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/sales-studio/agents/deal-scorer.md))
- **deal-scout**: Read-only shopping research for a product requirement across Amazon.in and Flipkart — reads search results, product pages, and the user's own cart / wishlist / saved-for-later, plus price-history sites and off-site review sources, and returns structured JSON. Never buys, never changes account state, never handles credentials. India only. ([abhisheksarkar30/claude-deal-scout](https://github.com/abhisheksarkar30/claude-deal-scout/blob/HEAD/agents/deal-scout.md))

([JeromyJSmith/deal-intelligence](https://github.com/JeromyJSmith/deal-intelligence/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/deal-scout.md))

- **dean**: Dean (Дін Вінчестер) — pytest test reviewer. Trusts nothing that only looks alive: assumes every green test is lying until it proves it can fail: runs the suite, walks each test down the review ladder (does the assert run, is the oracle independent, is the real unit exercised, does it tell a correct implementation from a plausible bug), backs weak-test claims with a mutation. Precision over recall. Reviews tests written by a session, a colleague, or his brother Sam; does not write or fix tests. - **debruijn**: The formalize lane's planner — de Bruijn, the Lean 4 engineer, on Opus. Launch it to plan a milestone's kernel module from its claim list (blind to the derivation until its plan is committed, then open): the statements read back against Mathlib, the skeleton with sorry at every leaf, a plan per leaf, outside theorems formalized or imported, and optimizations and generalizations reported at once. Also to open a kernel milestone (a formalization backlog, a port). The implementer (debruijn-implementer) builds what it plans. Not for deriving mathematics (ephodos) or posing questions (poros). ([kooshi/agentic-mathematics](https://github.com/kooshi/agentic-mathematics/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/debruijn.md) · ★1)
- **debruijn-implementer**: The formalize lane's implementer — de Bruijn, the Lean 4 engineer, on Opus 5.5. Launch it to build a kernel module from the planner's skeleton and plan (and the derivation behind it): replace every sorry with a proof without changing any statement, run the full gate, and draft the front-page lines that close the milestone. Also for project tooling jobs in tools/ and scripts/. Not for planning a module (debruijn), deriving (ephodos), or changing a statement — a statement it cannot prove goes back through Keryx. ([kooshi/hadwiger-nelson](https://github.com/kooshi/hadwiger-nelson/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/debruijn-implementer.md) · ★2)
- **debruijn-implementer**: The formalize lane's implementer — de Bruijn, the Lean 4 engineer, on Opus.
